QT (Quality Threshold) clustering is an algorithm that builds clusters\n// by starting with a seed point and iteratively adding the closest point\n// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <limits>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\n\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n    \n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n    \n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n        \n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n        \n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n            \n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            \n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// Find the closest unclustered point to the current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\n// Returns -1 if no such point exists\nint findClosestPoint(const std::vector<int>& cluster_members,\n                     const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\n                     const std::vector<bool>& in_cluster,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold,\n                     const int point_count) {\n    int closest_point = -1;\n    double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();\n    \n    // Try each unclustered point as a candidate\n    for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count; ++candidate) {\n        // Skip if already clustered or already in this cluster\n        if (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;\n        \n        // Calculate the maximum distance from candidate to all cluster members\n        double max_dist = 0.0;\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = cluster_members[i];\n            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);\n            max_dist = std::max(max_dist, dist);\n        }\n        \n        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best\n        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {\n            min_diameter = max_dist;\n            closest_point = candidate;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return closest_point;\n}\n\n// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point\n// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster\nint generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,\n                              const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\n                              const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                              const double threshold,\n                              const int point_count,\n                              std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {\n    std::vector<bool> in_cluster(point_count, false);\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    \n    // Add seed point\n    in_cluster[seed_point] = true;\n maintains diameter < threshold\n        const int closest = findClosestPoint(members, clustered, in_cluster, points, \n                                             threshold, point_count);\n        \n std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    \n    // Initialize unclustered indices\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\n    }\n    \n    // Main clustering loop\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\n        int max_cardinality = -1;\n        int best_seed = -1;\n        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;\n cluster, add it\n        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {\n            Cluster cluster;\n            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;\n            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;\n            clusters.push_back(cluster);\n            \n            // Mark all members as clustered\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;\n            }\n            \n            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list\n            unclustered_indices.erase(\n                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),\n                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),\n                unclustered_indices.end()\n            );\n        } else {\n            // No more clusters can be formed\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return clusters;\n}\n\n// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT double max_diameter = 0.0;\n        \n        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\n            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {\n                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]], \n                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);\n const int point_count,\n                              std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {\n    std::vector<bool> in_cluster(point_count, false);\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    \n    //            threshold, point_count);\n        \n        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added\n        \n        in_cluster[closest] = true;\n        members.push_back(closest);\n    }\n    \n    // Copy members if requested\n    if (cluster_members) {\n        *cluster_members = members;\n    }\n    \n    return static_cast<int>(members.size());\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    \n    // Initialize unclustered indices\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\n    }\n    \n    // Main clustering loop\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\n        int max_cardinality = -1;\n        int best_seed = -1;\n        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;\n        \n        // Try each unclustered point as a seed\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {\n            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];\n            if (clustered[seed]) continue;\n            \n            std::vector<int> candidate_members;\n            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, \n                                                       threshold, N, \n                                                       &candidate_members);\n            \n            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {\n                max_cardinality = cardinality;\n                best_seed = seed;\n                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;\n            }\n        }\n        \n        // If we found a cluster, add it\n        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {\n            Cluster cluster;\n            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;\n            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;\n            clusters.push_back(cluster);\n            \n            // Mark all members as clustered\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;\n            }\n            \n            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list\n            unclustered_indices.erase(\n                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),\n                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),\n                unclustered_indices.end()\n            );\n        } else {\n            // No more clusters can be formed\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return clusters;\n}\n\n// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties\nbool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold) {\n    bool valid = true;\n    \n    printf(\"Validating clusters:\\n\");\n    \n    // Check each cluster\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];\n        double max_diameter = 0.0;\n        \n        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\n            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {\n                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]], \n                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);\n                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);\n            }\n        }\n        \n        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters\n            printf(\"  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\\n\", \n                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);\n        }\n        \n        // Validate diameter is within threshold\n        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error\n            printf(\"ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\\n\", \n                   c, max_diameter, threshold);\n            valid = false;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Check for duplicate memberships\n    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = clusters[c].members[i];\n            if (membership[member] >= 0) {\n                printf(\"ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\\n\",\n                       member, membership[member], c);\n                valid = false;\n            }\n            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Count clustered points\n    int clustered_count = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < membership.size(); ++i) {\n        if (membership[i] >= 0) clustered_count++;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Total points: %zu, Clustered: %d, Unclustered: %zu\\n\",\n           points.size(), clustered_count, points.size() - clustered_count);\n    \n    return valid;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n      } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5-5","id":"msg_011CfiqC8qTkXHUGDRqNjWWS","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_018QPGSgK8mR7b2ZnBxAz2bs","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"cat > /tmp/qt/new_core.py <<'EOF'\nEOF\ncd qtclustering && python3 - <<'EOF'\nsrc = open('qtclustering.cpp').read()\nstart = src.index('// Find the closest unclustered point')\nend = src.index('// Validation: check that clusters')\nnew_core = r'''// Spatial grid used to accelerate neighbor queries. Cells are at least\n// `threshold` wide, so every point within distance < threshold of a point\n// lies in the 3x3 block of cells around it.\nstruct Grid {\n    int nx = 1, ny = 1;\n    double cell = 1.0;\n    std::vector<int> start;   // CSR offsets, size nx*ny+1\n    std::vector<int> items;   // point indices, ascending within each cell\n    std::vector<int> cell_of; // cell id of each point\n\n    void build(const std::vector<Point>& points, const double threshold) {\n        const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n        const int MAX_CELLS = 2048;\n        cell = std::max(threshold * 1.0001, std::max(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT) / MAX_CELLS);\n        nx = std::max(1, std::min(MAX_CELLS, static_cast<int>(std::ceil(MAX_WIDTH / cell)) + 1));\n        ny = std::max(1, std::min(MAX_CELLS, static_cast<int>(std::ceil(MAX_HEIGHT / cell)) + 1));\n        start.assign(static_cast<size_t>(nx) * ny + 1, 0);\n        cell_of.resize(N);\n        for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n            int cx = static_cast<int>(points[i].x / cell);\n            int cy = static_cast<int>(points[i].y / cell);\n            cx = std::min(std::max(cx, 0), nx - 1);\n            cy = std::min(std::max(cy, 0), ny - 1);\n            cell_of[i] = cy * nx + cx;\n            start[cell_of[i] + 1]++;\n        }\n        for (size_t c = 0; c + 1 < start.size(); ++c) start[c + 1] += start[c];\n        items.resize(N);\n        std::vector<int> pos(start.begin(), start.end() - 1);\n        for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) items[pos[cell_of[i]]++] = i;\n    }\n\n    template <class F>\n    void forNeighborCells(const int p, F&& f) const {\n        const int cx = cell_of[p] % nx;\n        const int cy = cell_of[p] / nx;\n        for (int y = std::max(cy - 1, 0); y <= std::min(cy + 1, ny - 1); ++y) {\n            for (int x = std::max(cx - 1, 0); x <= std::min(cx + 1, nx - 1); ++x) {\n                const int c = y * nx + x;\n                for (int k = start[c]; k < start[c + 1]; ++k) f(items[k]);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n};\n\n// Scratch buffers for candidate cluster generation (structure of arrays)\nstruct Workspace {\n    std::vector<int> idx;\n    std::vector<double> cx, cy, maxd;\n};\n\n// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point.\n// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster.\n//\n// Equivalent to the original greedy procedure: at each step the unclustered\n// point with the smallest maximum distance to the current members (ties ->\n// lowest index) is added as long as that distance is < threshold. The\n// maximum distance of every candidate is maintained incrementally, and\n// candidates whose maximum distance reaches the threshold are dropped\n// permanently (it can only grow).\nint generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,\n                             const std::vector<char>& clustered,\n                             const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                             const Grid& grid,\n                             const double threshold,\n                             Workspace& ws,\n                             std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {\n    ws.idx.clear();\n    grid.forNeighborCells(seed_point, [&](int q) {\n        if (q == seed_point || clustered[q]) return;\n        if (distance(points[q], points[seed_point]) < threshold) ws.idx.push_back(q);\n    });\n    std::sort(ws.idx.begin(), ws.idx.end());\n\n    int n = static_cast<int>(ws.idx.size());\n    ws.cx.resize(n);\n    ws.cy.resize(n);\n    ws.maxd.resize(n);\n    int* idx = ws.idx.data();\n    double* px = ws.cx.data();\n    double* py = ws.cy.data();\n    double* md = ws.maxd.data();\n    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        px[i] = points[idx[i]].x;\n        py[i] = points[idx[i]].y;\n        md[i] = distance(points[idx[i]], points[seed_point]);\n    }\n\n    if (cluster_members) {\n        cluster_members->clear();\n        cluster_members->push_back(seed_point);\n    }\n    int size = 1;\n\n    while (n > 0) {\n        // Select candidate with minimal max distance (first index on ties)\n        int best = 0;\n        double bestd = md[0];\n        for (int i = 1; i < n; ++i) {\n            if (md[i] < bestd) { bestd = md[i]; best = i; }\n        }\n        const double bx = px[best], by = py[best];\n        if (cluster_members) cluster_members->push_back(idx[best]);\n        ++size;\n\n        // Update max distances w.r.t. the new member, compacting in order\n        int m = 0;\n        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n            if (i == best) continue;\n            const double dx = px[i] - bx;\n            const double dy = py[i] - by;\n            const double d = std::max(md[i], std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy));\n            if (d < threshold) {\n                idx[m] = idx[i]; px[m] = px[i]; py[m] = py[i]; md[m] = d;\n                ++m;\n            }\n        }\n        n = m;\n    }\n    return size;\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm (MPI-parallel: seeds are distributed\n// cyclically over ranks; every rank ends up with the full result).\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    int rank = 0, nprocs = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &nprocs);\n\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<char> clustered(N, 0);\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n\n    Grid grid;\n    grid.build(points, threshold);\n    Workspace ws;\n\n    // Seeds owned by this rank, and their cached candidate cardinalities.\n    std::vector<int> my_seeds;\n    for (int s = rank; s < N; s += nprocs) my_seeds.push_back(s);\n    std::vector<int> card(my_seeds.size(), 0);\n    std::vector<char> dirty(my_seeds.size(), 1);\n    std::vector<int> dirty_list(my_seeds.size());\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < my_seeds.size(); ++i) dirty_list[i] = static_cast<int>(i);\n    std::vector<int> alive(my_seeds.size());\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < my_seeds.size(); ++i) alive[i] = static_cast<int>(i);\n\n    int remaining = N;\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    while (remaining > 0) {\n        // Recompute cardinalities of seeds whose neighborhood changed\n        for (int li : dirty_list) {\n            card[li] = generateCandidateCluster(my_seeds[li], clustered, points, grid,\n                                                threshold, ws);\n            dirty[li] = 0;\n        }\n        dirty_list.clear();\n\n        // Local best: max cardinality, smallest seed on ties\n        struct { int val; int loc; } local{-1, std::numeric_limits<int>::max()}, global;\n        for (int li : alive) {\n            if (card[li] > local.val) { local.val = card[li]; local.loc = my_seeds[li]; }\n        }\n        MPI_Allreduce(&local, &global, 1, MPI_2INT, MPI_MAXLOC, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n        if (global.val <= 0) break;\n        const int best_seed = global.loc;\n        const int max_card = global.val;\n\n        members.resize(max_card);\n        if (best_seed % nprocs == rank) {\n            generateCandidateCluster(best_seed, clustered, points, grid, threshold, ws,\n                                     &members);\n        }\n        MPI_Bcast(members.data(), max_card, MPI_INT, best_seed % nprocs, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n        Cluster cluster;\n        cluster.seed_point = best_seed;\n        cluster.members = members;\n        clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));\n\n        for (int p : members) clustered[p] = 1;\n        remaining -= max_card;\n\n        // Invalidate owned seeds that had a newly clustered point as candidate\n        for (int p : members) {\n            grid.forNeighborCells(p, [&](int q) {\n                if (clustered[q] || q % nprocs != rank) return;\n                const int li = q / nprocs;\n                if (dirty[li]) return;\n                if (distance(points[q], points[p]) < threshold) {\n                    dirty[li] = 1;\n                    dirty_list.push_back(li);\n                }\n            });\n        }\n        alive.erase(std::remove_if(alive.begin(), alive.end(),\n                                   [&](int li) { return clustered[my_seeds[li]] != 0; For each seed we keep\n    // either its exact candidate cardinality (clean) or, if its neighborhood\n    // changed since the last evaluation (dirty), the upper bound\n    // 1 + #unclustered neighbors within threshold. Dirty seeds are evaluated\n    // lazily, only when their bound could beat the current best.\n    const int L = (N > rank) ? (N - rank + nprocs - 1) / nprocs : 0;\n    std::vector<int> card(L, 0);\n    std::vector<int> nb(L, 0);\n    std::vector<char> dirty(L, 1);\n    std::vector<int> alive(L);\n    for (int li = 0; li < L; ++li) {\n        alive[li] = li;\n        const int s = rank + li * nprocs;\n        int cnt = 0;\n        grid.forNeighborCells(s, [&](int q) {\n            if (q != s && distance(points[q], points[s]) < threshold) ++cnt;\n        });\n        nb[li] = cnt;\n    }\n\n    struct ValLoc { int val; int loc; };\n    // true if (v, s) beats b: larger cardinality, or equal with smaller seed\n    auto beats = [](int v, int s, const ValLoc& b) {\n        return v > b.val || (v == b.val && s < b.loc);\n    };\n\n    std::vector<int> pending;\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int remaining = N;\n    while (remaining > 0) {\n        // Phase 1: best among clean (exactly known) seeds\n        ValLoc local{-1, std::numeric_limits<int>::max()}, global;\n        for (int li : alive) {\n            if (!dirty[li] && beats(card[li], rank + li * nprocs, local)) {\n                local = {card[li], rank + li * nprocs};\n            }\n        }\n        MPI_Allreduce(&local, &global, 1, MPI_2INT, MPI_MAXLOC, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n        // Phase 2: evaluate dirty seeds whose bound could beat the best\n        pending.clear();\n        for (int li : alive) {\n            if (dirty[li] && beats(nb[li] + 1, rank + li * nprocs, global)) pending.push_back(li);\n        }\n        std::sort(pending.begin(), pending.end(), [&](int x, int y) {\n            return nb[x] != nb[y] ? nb[x] > nb[y] : x < y;\n        });\n        ValLoc cur = beats(local.val, local.loc, global) ? local : global;\n        for (int li : pending) {\n            const int s = rank + li * nprocs;\n            if (!beats(nb[li] + 1, s, cur)) break;\n            card[li] = generateCandidateCluster(s, clustered, points, grid, threshold, ws);\n            dirty[li] = 0;\n            if (beats(card[li], s, local)) local = {card[li], s};\n            if (beats(card[li], s, cur)) cur = {card[li], s};\n        }\n        MPI_Allreduce(&local, &global, 1, MPI_2INT, MPI_MAXLOC, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n        if (global.val <= 0) break;\n        const int best_seed = global.loc;\n        const int max_card = global.val;\n        const int owner = best_seed % nprocs;\n\n        members.resize(max_card);\n        if (owner == rank) {\n            generateCandidateCluster(best_seed, clustered, points, grid, threshold, ws,\n                                     &members);\n        }\n        MPI_Bcast(members.data(), max_card, MPI_INT, owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n        Cluster cluster;\n        cluster.seed_point = best_seed;\n        cluster.members = members;\n        clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));\n\n        for (int p : members) clustered[p] = 1;\n        remaining -= max_card;\n\n        // Owned unclustered seeds that lose a neighbor become dirty\n        for (int p : members) {\n            grid.forNeighborCells(p, [&](int q) {\n                if (clustered[q] || q % nprocs != rank) return;\n                if (distance(points[q], points[p]) < threshold) {\n                    const int li = q / nprocs;\n                    nb[li]--;\n                    dirty[li] = 1;\n                }\n            });\n        }\n        alive.erase(std::remove_if(alive.begin(), alive.end(),\n                                   [&](int li) { return clustered[rank + li * nprocs] != 0; }),\n                    alive.end());\n    }\n\n'''\nsrc = src[:a] + new + src[b:]\nopen('qtclustering.cpp','w').write(src)\nEOF\nmake -C /tmp/qt/build 2>&1 | grep -E \"warning|error|Built\"; cd /tmp/qt; for args in \"-n 1000\" \"-n 3000\" The\n// maximum (squared) distance of every candidate is maintained\n// incrementally, and candidates whose maximum distance reaches the\n// threshold are dropped permanently (it can only grow).\nint generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,\n                             const std::vector<char>& clustered,\n                             const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                             const Grid& grid,\n                             const double thr2, // sqrtLowerCut(threshold)\n                             Workspace& ws,\n                             std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {\n    const double sx = points[seed_point].x, sy = points[seed_point].y;\n    ws.idx.clear();\n    grid.forNeighborCells(seed_point, [&](int q) {\n        if (q == seed_point || clustered[q]) return;\n        if (sqDist(points[q].x, points[q].y, sx, sy) < thr2) ws.idx.push_back(q);\n    });\n    std::sort(ws.idx.begin(), ws.idx.end());\n\n    int n = static_cast<int>(ws.idx.size());\n    ws.cx.resize(n);\n    ws.cy.resize(n);\n    ws.md2.resize(n);\n    int* __restrict idx = ws.idx.data();\n    double* __restrict px = ws.cx.data();\n    double* __restrict py = ws.cy.data();\n    double* __restrict md = ws.md2.data();\n    double mn = DEAD;\n    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        px[i] = points[idx[i]].x;\n        py[i] = points[idx[i]].y;\n        md[i] = sqDist(px[i], py[i], sx, sy);\n        mn = md[i] < mn ? md[i] : mn;\n    }\n\n    if (cluster_members) {\n        cluster_members->clear();\n        cluster_members->push_back(seed_point);\n    }\n    int size = 1;\n\n    while (mn < thr2) {\n        // Select candidate with minimal max distance (first index on ties of\n        // the rounded distance, i.e. squared values in [mn, upper))\n        const double upper = sqrtUpperCut(mn);\n        int best = 0;\n        while (!(md[best] < upper)) ++best;\n        const double bx = px[best], by = py[best];\n        if (cluster_members) cluster_members->push_back(idx[best]);\n        md[best] = DEAD;\n        ++size;\n\n        // Update max distances w.r.t. the new member; drop exceeded ones\n        double m0 = DEAD, m1 = DEAD, m2 = DEAD, m3 = DEAD;\n        int alive = 0;\n        int i = 0;\n        for (; i + 4 <= n; i += 4) {\n            double v0 = std::max(md[i + 0], sqDist(px[i + 0], py[i + 0], bx, by));\n            double v1 = std::max(md[i + 1], sqDist(px[i + 1], py[i + 1], bx, by));\n            double v2 = std::max(md[i + 2], sqDist(px[i + 2], py[i + 2], bx, by));\n            double v3 = std::max(md[i + 3], sqDist(px[i + 3], py[i + 3], bx, by));\n            v0 = v0 < thr2 ? v0 : DEAD;\n            v1 = v1 < thr2 ? v1 : DEAD;\n            v2 = v2 < thr2 ? v2 : DEAD;\n            v3 = v3 < thr2 ? v3 : DEAD;\n            md[i + 0] = v0; md[i + 1] = v1; md[i + 2] = v2; md[i + 3] = v3;\n            m0 = v0 < m0 ? v0 : m0;\n            m1 = v1 < m1 ? v1 : m1;\n            m2 = v2 < m2 ? v2 : m2;\n            m3 = v3 < m3 ? v3 : m3;\n            alive += (v0 < DEAD) + (v1 < DEAD) + (v2 < DEAD) + (v3 < DEAD);\n        }\n        for (; i < n; ++i) {\n            double v = std::max(md[i], sqDist(px[i], py[i], bx, by));\n            v = v < thr2 ? v : DEAD;\n            md[i] = v;\n            m0 = v < m0 ? v : m0;\n            alive += (v < DEAD);\n        }\n        mn = std::min(std::min(m0, m1), std::min(m2, m3));\n\n        // Compact (order preserving) once many entries are dead\n        if (alive * 2 < n) {\n            int m = 0;\n            for (int j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n                if (md[j] < DEAD) {\n                    idx[m] = idx[j]; px[m] = px[j]; py[m] = py[j]; md[m] = md[j];\n                    ++m;\n                }\n            }\n            n = m;\n        }\n    }\n    return size;\n}\n\n'''\nsrc = src[:a] + new + src[b:]\n# qtClustering: use thr2\nsrc = src.replace('''    Grid grid;\n    grid.build(points, threshold);\n    Workspace ws;''', '''    Grid grid;\n    grid.build(points, threshold);\n    Workspace ws;\n    const double thr2 = sqrtLowerCut(threshold);''')\nsrc = src.replace('generateCandidateCluster(s, clustered, points, grid, threshold, ws)', 'generateCandidateCluster(s, clustered, points, grid, thr2, ws)')\nsrc = src.replace('generateCandidateCluster(best_seed, clustered, points, grid, threshold, ws,', 'generateCandidateCluster(best_seed, clustered, points, grid, thr2, ws,')\nopen('qtclustering.cpp','w').write(src)\nEOF\ngrep -n \"threshold\" qtclustering.cpp | sed -n 1,200p | grep -v printf | awk -F: '$1>200 && $1<320'"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1530,"cache_read_input_tokens":44669,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1530},"output_tokens":20,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"28fa033e-3fb0-46ba-ad3a-00b381ebeda7","uuid":"feec27dc-9b5c-42c0-bebb-40d951db3b7c","timestamp":"2026-10-05T09:48:59.663Z","request_id":"req_011Cfit1HjYhhJgiQemkPi79"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5-5","id":"msg_011Cfit8XKoXtRHsW8o8TQBr","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_018hE1VRVcuwMxwoFAn6j8Gh","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"cd qtclustering && python3 - <<'EOF'\nsrc = open('qtclustering.cpp').read()\na = src.index('static inline double sqDist(')\nb = src.index('// Main QT clustering algorithm (MPI')\nnew = r'''static inline double sqDist(const double ax, const double ay, const double bx, const double by) {\n    const double dx = ax - bx;\n    const double dy = ay - by;\n    return dx * dx + dy * dy;\n}\n\n// 4-wide double vectors (GCC/Clang vector extension)\ntypedef double v4d __attribute__((vector_size(32)));\ntypedef long long v4l __attribute__((vector_size(32)));\nstatic const int VW = 4;\n\nstatic inline v4d vload(const double* p) {\n    v4d v;\n    std::memcpy(&v, p, sizeof(v));\n    return v;\n}\nstatic inline void vstore(double* p, const v4d v) { std::memcpy(p, &v, sizeof(v)); }\nstatic inline v4d vsplat(const double x) { return v4d{x, x, x, x}; }\n\n// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point.\n// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster.\n//\n// Equivalent to the original greedy procedure: at each step the unclustered\n// point with the smallest maximum distance to the current members (ties ->\n// lowest index) is added as long as that distance is < threshold. The\n// maximum (squared) distance of every candidate is maintained\n// incrementally, and candidates whose maximum distance reaches the\n// threshold are dropped permanently (it can only grow).\nint generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,\n                             const std::vector<char>& clustered,\n                             const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                             const Grid& grid,\n                             const double thr2, // sqrtLowerCut(threshold)\n                             Workspace& ws,\n                             std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {\n    const double sx = points[seed_point].x, sy = points[seed_point].y;\n    ws.idx.clear();\n    grid.forNeighborCells(seed_point, [&](int q) {\n        if (q == seed_point || clustered[q]) return;\n        if (sqDist(points[q].x, points[q].y, sx, sy) < thr2) ws.idx.push_back(q);\n    });\n    std::sort(ws.idx.begin(), ws.idx.end());\n\n    int n = static_cast<int>(ws.idx.size());\n    const int cap = (n + VW - 1) / VW * VW;\n    ws.idx.resize(cap, -1);\n    ws.cx.resize(cap);\n    ws.cy.resize(cap);\n    ws.md2.resize(cap);\n    int* __restrict idx = ws.idx.data();\n    double* __restrict px = ws.cx.data();\n    double* __restrict py = ws.cy.data();\n    double* __restrict md = ws.md2.data();\n    double mn = DEAD;\n    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        px[i] = points[idx[i]].x;\n        py[i] = points[idx[i]].y;\n        md[i] = sqDist(px[i], py[i], sx, sy);\n        mn = md[i] < mn ? md[i] : mn;\n    }\n    // Padding entries are permanently dead\n    for (int i = n; i < cap; ++i) { px[i] = 0.0; py[i] = 0.0; md[i] = DEAD; }\n    n = cap;\n\n    if (cluster_members) {\n        cluster_members->clear();\n        cluster_members->push_back(seed_point);\n    }\n    int size = 1;\n\n    const v4d vthr = vsplat(thr2);\n    const v4d vdead = vsplat(DEAD);\n    while (mn < thr2) {\n        // Select candidate with minimal max distance (first index on ties of\n        // the rounded distance, i.e. squared values in [mn, upper))\n        const double upper = sqrtUpperCut(mn);\n        int best = 0;\n        for (;; {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01SGKc5WmS9iVH5NZP3SZTRA","type":"tool_result","content":"std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    int rank = 0, nprocs = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &nprocs);\n\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<char> clustered(N, 0);\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n\n    Grid grid, my_grid; // all points / seeds owned by this rank\n    grid.build(points, threshold);\n    my_grid.build(points, threshold, nprocs, rank);\n    Workspace ws;\n    const double thr2 = sqrtLowerCut(threshold);\n\n    // Seeds owned by this rank (cyclic distribution). For each seed we keep\n    // either its exact candidate cardinality (exact) or an upper bound on it\n    // (initially 1 + #unclustered neighbors within threshold). Inexact seeds\n    // are evaluated lazily, only when their bound could beat the current best.\n    const int L = (N > rank) ? (N - rank + nprocs - 1) / nprocs : 0;\n    std::vector<int> card(L, 0); // exact cardinality or upper bound\n    std::vector<int> nb(L, 0);   // #unclustered neighbors within threshold\n    std::vector<char> dirty(L, 1);\n    std::vector<int> alive(L);\n    for (int li = 0; li < L; ++li) {\n        alive[li] = li;\n        const int s = rank + li * nprocs;\n        int cnt = 0;\n        grid.forNeighborCells(s, [&](int q) {\n            if (q != s && distance(points[q], points[s]) < threshold) ++cnt;\n        });\n        nb[li] = cnt;\n        card[li] = cnt + 1;\n    }\n\n    struct ValLoc { int val; int loc; };\n    // true if (v, s) beats b: larger cardinality, or equal with smaller seed\n    auto beats = [](int v, int s, const ValLoc& b) {\n        return v > b.val || (v == b.val && s < b.loc);\n    };\n\n    std::vector<int> pending;\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int remaining = N;\n    while (remaining > 0) {\n        // Phase 1: best among clean (exactly known) seeds\n        ValLoc local{-1, std::numeric_limits<int>::max()}, global;\n        for (int li : alive) {\n            if (!dirty[li] && beats(card[li], rank + li * nprocs, local)) {\n                local = {card[li], rank + li * nprocs};\n            }\n        }\n        MPI_Allreduce(&local, &global, 1, MPI_2INT, MPI_MAXLOC, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n        // Phase 2: evaluate dirty seeds whose bound could beat the best\n        pending.clear();\n        for (int li : alive) {\n            if (dirty[li] && beats(card[li], rank + li * nprocs, global)) pending.push_back(li);\n        }\n        std::sort(pending.begin(), pending.end(), [&](int x, int y) {\n            return card[x] != card[y] ?
